## FINH-2478 [必须]
判据: Response recommends using key rate duration (KRD) allocation across different tenor underlyings (such as 5y/7y/10y) to minimize curve basis risk when structuring the payer swaption hedge.
提示: Key rate duration matching is essential for sophisticated interest rate hedging as it addresses curve risk beyond simple parallel shifts. Callable bond portfolios have complex duration profiles across the yield curve, and proper KRD allocation ensures the hedge remains effective under non-parallel rate movements. This demonstrates expert-level fixed income risk management.
出处: prbench:61139c8c4221f9b8833aea88 | 话题: Derivatives & Structured Products

## FINH-2479 [必须]
判据: Response recommends payer spreads (buying lower-strike payers, selling higher-strike payers) rather than receiver-payer collars for cost reduction.
提示: Payer spreads maintain directional consistency while reducing costs, demonstrating sophisticated cost management in derivatives structuring without creating conflicting exposures.
出处: prbench:61139c8c4221f9b8833aea88 | 话题: Derivatives & Structured Products

## FINH-2480 [必须]
判据: Response recommends cancellable (Bermudan) pay-fixed swaps to avoid over-hedging when bond durations collapse due to call exercises.
提示: Cancellable swaps are a sophisticated solution that directly addresses the negative convexity problem by allowing the hedge to be unwound when the underlying bonds' effective duration collapses. This demonstrates expert-level understanding of how to manage asymmetric risks in callable bond portfolios.
出处: prbench:61139c8c4221f9b8833aea88 | 话题: Derivatives & Structured Products

## FINH-2481 [必须]
判据: Response recommends using mid-curve tenor swaptions (such as 1y7y, 2y6y) that better match the portfolio's key rate duration risk rather than short-dated 1y1y or 2y1y options.
提示: Matching the hedge tenor to the portfolio's key rate exposures minimizes basis risk and provides more effective hedging. Using 1y1y options to hedge 8-year average maturity bonds creates significant tenor mismatch.
出处: prbench:61139c8c4221f9b8833aea88 | 话题: Derivatives & Structured Products

## FINH-2482 [必须]
判据: Response recommends payer swaptions or payer-based structures as the primary hedge against rising interest rates in this scenario.
提示: Payer swaptions are the correct instrument for rising rate protection, as they gain value when rates rise above the strike. This demonstrates proper understanding of swaption mechanics and appropriate hedging instrument selection for the client's specific risk exposure.
出处: prbench:61139c8c4221f9b8833aea88 | 话题: Derivatives & Structured Products
